実装範囲：
1) 1号機を座標受付・全位置履歴の永続化の所有者にする。通常座標更新は1号機のWALへの完了後に成功応答し、履歴を省略/集約で消さない。
2) 3号機を利用者・ライド作成/制御・配車・決済・通知・nearbyなど制御/参照APIと制御WALの所有者にする。nginx正規routing、role/Taskfile・deploy設定、systemd/envの必要変更までローカルで実装し、配布可能な構成にする。単にsolverだけ移設して案1全体の実装完了とはしない。
3) 毎座標更新をRPCとして3号機へ転送して待つ旧wed方式は避ける。dirtyな最新位置/距離は一括投影し、到着・配車・ステータス・完了のイベントは順序付きで同期。普通のnearby/owner座標・距離には公式3秒以内の反映を維持。到着判定・status更新・割当・ACK gatingには適切な同期境界を設ける。
4) 二つのWAL・init generation・chair/event sequenceを整合させる。受理後の通常service/OS再起動から復元、未配送イベントの再送、重複適用防止を実装。到着座標とPICKUP/ARRIVEDの対応を確定して公開。片側だけが進んだ時の回復を必要な正当性の範囲で検証。強制リセット/電源断用の対策は追加しない。
5) 通知ACKはrecipient単位の送達状態として制御状態の共通ロックから分離し、書込みをまとめる。recipientごとに大量のWALファイルを作ることを必須とはせず適切なshard/batch構造でよい。順序とat least once/Last-Event-ID replay、Flush成功後のACK、COMPLETED通知前の再配車禁止、椅子availability gatingを維持。3へ移すだけで共通ロックの競合が残る構造にしない。
6) スキーマ/配置/不要コード削除等、依頼実現に必要な破壊的変更はAGENTSで許可済み。競技DB初期化は既存db-recreate/公式initializeの経路を使える構成を作る。旧コード互換併存/rollback専用flagやmigrationは不要。役割を指定する設定は必要な分割構成として可。競技固有task/toolsはTaskfile.contest.yml/tools/contestへ。generated upstreamは手編集不可。

既存計測を新role配置へ正しく対応させる必要な設定は行ってよいが、新しい計測カウンター/ログ/traceをこの依頼だけで追加しない。保存済みの証拠で評価し、性能改善は未確定と報告する。
GoはLinux/amd64ローカルcross build。意味のある最小限の正当性/race/2ownerイベント順序・再送・restart/init・通知/到着/再配車テストと既存Taskfile上の必要checksを実施し、英語commit。ユーザーは実装を依頼済みなので通常の設計選択で再確認して止まらず、必要なローカル実装を完成させる。作業記録ファイルは通常追加不要。性能確認は同一RUN同時間窓の1/3CPU増減、座標応答、制御イベント/ACK遅延、実移動・再利用、完了・scoreを単独で測る。

sed -n '55,85p' webapp/go/runtime_state.go; cat > webapp/go/runtime_delivery.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
 "context"
 "errors"
 "fmt"
 "io"
 "os"
 "path/filepath"
 "sync"
 "sync/atomic"
 "time"
)

const deliveryStripeCount = 8

type deliveryEntry struct {
 queue *notificationQueue
 recipient string
 sent atomic.Pointer[time.Time]
}
type deliveryRequest struct { ack notificationAck; entry *deliveryEntry; done chan error }
type deliveryStripe struct {
 wal *os.File
 sequence uint64
 requests chan deliveryRequest
 stop chan struct{}
 done chan struct{}
}
type deliveryState struct {
 state *rideState
 entries sync.Map
 stripes [deliveryStripeCount]deliveryStripe
 generation string
}

// Recipient stripes append batches independently of the ride mutation mutex.
// The complete queue is retained; only delivery state is changed after a write.
func openDeliveryState(directory string) (*deliveryState, error) {
 d := &deliveryState{}
 for i := range d.stripes {
  f, err := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(directory, fmt.Sprintf("delivery-%d.wal", i)), os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0600)
  if err != nil { d.close(); return nil, err }
  stripe := &d.stripes[i]
  stripe.wal, stripe.requests, stripe.stop, stripe.done = f, make(chan deliveryRequest, 1024), make(chan struct{}), make(chan struct{})
  go d.run(stripe)
 }
 return d, nil
}
func deliveryKey(kind,id string) string { return kind+":"+id }
func (d *deliveryState) reset() { d.entries = sync.Map{} }
func (d *deliveryState) register(kind, recipient string, status RideStatus, q *notificationQueue) {
 entry := &deliveryEntry{queue:q, recipient:recipient}
 at := status.AppSentAt
 if kind == "chair" { at = status.ChairSentAt }
 entry.sent.Store(at)
 d.entries.Store(deliveryKey(kind,status.ID),entry)
}
func (s *rideState) notificationSent(kind,id string) bool {
 if s.acks != nil {
  if value,ok := s.acks.entries.Load(deliveryKey(kind,id)); ok { return value.(*deliveryEntry).sent.Load()!=nil }
 }
 status := s.statuses[id]
 if kind=="chair" { return status.ChairSentAt != nil }
 return status.AppSentAt != nil
}
func (d *deliveryState) acknowledge(ctx context.Context, kind,id string) error {
 if kind!="chair" && kind!="app" { return errors.New("invalid notification recipient kind") }
 value,ok := d.entries.Load(deliveryKey(kind,id))
 if !ok { return errors.New("notification status not found") }
 entry:=value.(*deliveryEntry)
 if entry.sent.Load()!=nil { return nil }
 req:=deliveryRequest{ack:notificationAck{Kind:kind,StatusID:id,At:time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Microsecond)},entry:entry,done:make(chan error,1)}
 stripe:=&d.stripes[coordinateStripeIndex(deliveryKey(kind,entry.recipient))]
 select { case stripe.requests<-req: case <-ctx.Done(): return ctx.Err() }
 select { case err:=<-req.done: return err; case <-ctx.Done(): return ctx.Err() }
}
func (d *deliveryState) run(stripe *deliveryStripe) {
 defer close(stripe.done)
 for {
  var first deliveryRequest
  select { case <-stripe.stop: return; case first = <-stripe.requests: }
  batch:=[]deliveryRequest{first}
  // A small bounded wait combines concurrent SSE flush acknowledgements.
  timer:=time.NewTimer(2*time.Millisecond)
 collect:
  for len(batch)<256 {
   select { case req:=<-stripe.requests: batch=append(batch,req); case <-timer.C: break collect }
  }
  if !timer.Stop() { select { case <-timer.C: default: } }
  acks:=make([]notificationAck,0,len(batch))
  for _,req:=range batch { if req.entry.sent.Load()==nil { acks=append(acks,req.ack) } }
  var err error
  if len(acks)>0 {
   err=writeWALRecord(stripe.wal,stateRecord{Version:1,Generation:d.generation,Sequence:stripe.sequence+1,Acks:acks})
   if err==nil { stripe.sequence++; for _,ack:=range acks { d.apply(ack) } }
  }
  for _,req:=range batch { req.done<-err }
 }
}
func (d *deliveryState) apply(ack notificationAck) {
 value,ok:=d.entries.Load(deliveryKey(ack.Kind,ack.StatusID))
 if !ok { return }
 entry:=value.(*deliveryEntry)
 at:=ack.At
 if !entry.sent.CompareAndSwap(nil,&at) { return }
 entry.queue.ack(ack.StatusID)
 if ack.Kind=="chair" { d.state.deliveryDirty.Store(entry.recipient,at) }
}

// Refresh only derived indexes and existing measurements at control boundaries.
// ACK workers never acquire s.mu or rewrite the immutable ride status rows.
func (s *rideState) refreshDeliveryIndexes() {
 s.deliveryDirty.Range(func(key,value any) bool {
  if !s.deliveryDirty.CompareAndDelete(key,value) { return true }
  id:=key.(string)
  if s.dispatch!=nil {
   queue:=s.chairEvents[id]
   pending:=0
   if queue!=nil { pending=int(queue.count.Load()) }
   s.dispatch.changeChair(id,func(c *chairMetricState){ c.pending=pending })
  }
  s.refreshChairIndexes(id)
  return true
 })
}
func (d *deliveryState) recover(generation string) error {
 d.generation=generation
 for i:=range d.stripes {
  stripe:=&d.stripes[i]
  stripe.sequence=0
  f:=stripe.wal
  if _,err:=f.Seek(0,io.SeekStart); err!=nil { return err }
  header,err:=readWALRecord(f)
  if errors.Is(err,io.EOF)||errors.Is(err,io.ErrUnexpectedEOF)||(err==nil&&header.Generation!=generation) {
   if err:=f.Truncate(0);err!=nil{return err}; if _,err:=f.Seek(0,io.SeekStart);err!=nil{return err}
   if err:=writeWALRecord(f,stateRecord{Version:1,Generation:generation});err!=nil{return err}
   continue
  }
  if err!=nil{return err}; if header.Sequence!=0{return errors.New("invalid delivery WAL header")}
  for {
   offset,err:=f.Seek(0,io.SeekCurrent);if err!=nil{return err}
   record,err:=readWALRecord(f)
   if errors.Is(err,io.EOF)||errors.Is(err,io.ErrUnexpectedEOF) {
    if err:=f.Truncate(offset);err!=nil{return err};if _,err:=f.Seek(offset,io.SeekStart);err!=nil{return err};break
   }
   if err!=nil{return err}
   if record.Generation!=generation||record.Sequence!=stripe.sequence+1{return errors.New("delivery WAL order mismatch")}
   stripe.sequence=record.Sequence
   for _,ack:=range record.Acks {
    value,ok:=d.entries.Load(deliveryKey(ack.Kind,ack.StatusID))
    if !ok || coordinateStripeIndex(deliveryKey(ack.Kind,value.(*deliveryEntry).recipient))!=i { return errors.New("delivery WAL recipient mismatch") }
    d.apply(ack)
   }
  }
 }
 return nil
}
func (d *deliveryState) sync() error {
 if d==nil{return nil}
 var result error
 for i:=range d.stripes { if d.stripes[i].wal!=nil {result=errors.Join(result,d.stripes[i].wal.Sync())} }
 return result
}
func (d *deliveryState) close() error {
 if d==nil{return nil}
 var result error
 for i:=range d.stripes {
  stripe:=&d.stripes[i]
  if stripe.wal==nil{continue}
  close(stripe.stop);<-stripe.done
  result=errors.Join(result,stripe.wal.Sync(),stripe.wal.Close())
 }
 return result
}
EOF

package main

import (
 "bytes"
 "context"
 "database/sql"
 "encoding/json"
 "errors"
 "fmt"
 "io"
 "net"
 "net/http"
 "os"
 "sync"
 "time"

 "github.com/jmoiron/sqlx"
 "github.com/oklog/ulid/v2"
)

// Public traffic uses :8080. Owner messages use a separate, private listener.
// No owner endpoint is registered on the public mux, including matcher traffic.
type ownerTransport struct {
 role, peerURL, peerIP, listen string
 client *http.Client
 life sync.RWMutex
 dirty sync.Map
 stop, done chan struct{}
}
type coordinateProjection struct { Distance distanceTotal; Sequence uint64 }
type ownerChair struct { Chair Chair; Route coordinateRoute; Initial distanceTotal }
type ownerSeed struct { Generation string; Chairs []ownerChair }
type ownerInstall struct { Generation string; Chairs []ownerChair }
type ownerStatusCommand struct { Generation, ChairID, RideID, Status string }
type ownerProjectionBatch struct { Generation string; Positions []coordinateProjection }
type ownerArrival struct { Generation string; Record coordinateRecord }

func configuredOwner() *ownerTransport {
 role:=os.Getenv("ISURIDE_OWNER")
 if role!="control" && role!="coordinate" { panic("ISURIDE_OWNER must be control or coordinate") }
 peer:=os.Getenv("ISURIDE_PEER_URL")
 listen:=os.Getenv("ISURIDE_OWNER_LISTEN")
 ip:=os.Getenv("ISURIDE_PEER_IP")
 if peer==""||listen==""||ip=="" {panic("owner peer URL, peer IP and private listener are required")}
 return &ownerTransport{role:role,peerURL:peer,peerIP:ip,listen:listen,client:&http.Client{Timeout:2*time.Second,Transport:&http.Transport{MaxIdleConnsPerHost:64}}}
}
func (o *ownerTransport) call(ctx context.Context, method,path string, input,output any) error {
 var data []byte
 var err error
 if input!=nil {data,err=json.Marshal(input);if err!=nil{return err}}
 for attempt:=0;attempt<2;attempt++ {
  request,err:=http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx,method,o.peerURL+path,bytes.NewReader(data));if err!=nil{return err}
  request.Header.Set("Content-Type","application/json")
  response,callErr:=o.client.Do(request)
  if callErr!=nil {err=callErr;continue}
  if response.StatusCode!=http.StatusOK {
   message,_:=io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(response.Body,4096));response.Body.Close()
   err=&stateHTTPError{code:response.StatusCode,message:string(message)}
   if response.StatusCode>=500 {continue};return err
  }
  if output!=nil {err=json.NewDecoder(response.Body).Decode(output)} else {_,err=io.Copy(io.Discard,response.Body)}
  response.Body.Close()
  if err==nil{return nil}
 }
 return err
}
func (s *rideState) ownerChair(id string) ownerChair {
 cell:=s.coordinateCell(id)
 cell.routeMu.RLock();route:=cell.route;cell.routeMu.RUnlock()
 return ownerChair{Chair:s.chairs[id],Route:route,Initial:s.initialDistances[id]}
}
func (s *rideState) ownerSeed() ownerSeed {
 s.mu.RLock();defer s.mu.RUnlock()
 seed:=ownerSeed{Generation:s.generation,Chairs:make([]ownerChair,0,len(s.chairs))}
 for id:=range s.chairs {seed.Chairs=append(seed.Chairs,s.ownerChair(id))}
 return seed
}
func (s *rideState) installChair(ctx context.Context,id string) error {
 if s.owner==nil {return nil}
 s.mu.RLock();request:=ownerInstall{Generation:s.generation,Chairs:[]ownerChair{s.ownerChair(id)}};s.mu.RUnlock()
 return s.owner.call(ctx,http.MethodPost,"/install",request,nil)
}
func (o *ownerTransport) chairStatus(s *rideState,chairID,rideID,status string) error {
 // Validate external requests before the retryable, idempotent internal command.
 s.mu.RLock()
 ride:=s.rides[rideID]
 var err error
 switch {
 case ride==nil:err=stateError(http.StatusNotFound,"ride not found")
 case ride.ChairID.String!=chairID:err=stateError(http.StatusBadRequest,"not assigned to this ride")
 case status=="ENROUTE" && s.latest(ride)!="MATCHING":err=stateError(http.StatusBadRequest,"ride already acknowledged")
 case status=="CARRYING" && s.latest(ride)!="PICKUP":err=stateError(http.StatusBadRequest,"chair has not arrived yet")
 case status!="ENROUTE" && status!="CARRYING":err=stateError(http.StatusBadRequest,"invalid status")
 }
 command:=ownerStatusCommand{Generation:s.generation,ChairID:chairID,RideID:rideID,Status:status}
 s.mu.RUnlock()
 if err!=nil{return err}
 return o.call(context.Background(),http.MethodPost,"/status",command,nil)
}

func newCoordinateOwner(ctx context.Context,database *sqlx.DB,directory string,owner *ownerTransport) (*rideState,error) {
 if err:=os.MkdirAll(directory,0700);err!=nil{return nil,err}
 coordinates,err:=openCoordinateState(directory);if err!=nil{return nil,err}
 s:=&rideState{database:database,coordinates:coordinates,owner:owner}
 if err:=s.loadCollation(ctx);err!=nil{coordinates.close();return nil,err}
 var seed ownerSeed
 // The control owner restores first. A coordinate restart never invents a
 // generation or drops its outbox merely because its peer is starting.
 for {
  if err=owner.call(ctx,http.MethodGet,"/seed",nil,&seed);err==nil {break}
  select {case <-ctx.Done():coordinates.close();return nil,ctx.Err();case <-time.After(100*time.Millisecond):}
 }
 if err:=s.resetCoordinateOwner(seed);err!=nil{coordinates.close();return nil,err}
 return s,nil
}
func (s *rideState) resetCoordinateOwner(seed ownerSeed) error {
 s.coordinateReady.Store(false)
 s.coordinates.reset()
 s.owner.dirty=sync.Map{}
 s.generation=seed.Generation
 for _,row:=range seed.Chairs {
  s.publishCoordinateChair(row.Chair)
  s.seedCoordinateDistance(row.Chair.ID,row.Initial)
  cell:=s.coordinateCell(row.Chair.ID);cell.route=row.Route
 }
 if err:=s.recoverCoordinates();err!=nil{return err}
 // Every recovered position is projected again. Arrival references in the
 // control WAL make resending the complete durable arrival outbox harmless.
 s.coordinates.cells.Range(func(key,value any) bool {cell:=value.(*coordinateCell);if cell.snapshot.Load().sequence>0{s.owner.dirty.Store(key,cell.snapshot.Load())};return true})
 s.coordinateFailed.Store(nil)
 s.coordinateReady.Store(true)
 return nil
}

func (s *rideState) coordinateOwned(chairID string,p Coordinate) (time.Time,error) {
 cell:=s.coordinateCell(chairID)
 if cell==nil||cell.chair.Load()==nil{return time.Time{},sql.ErrNoRows}
 cell.mu.Lock();defer cell.mu.Unlock()
 if err:=s.coordinateAvailable();err!=nil{return time.Time{},err}
 if err:=s.deliverArrivals(cell);err!=nil{return time.Time{},err}
 cell.routeMu.RLock();route:=cell.route;cell.routeMu.RUnlock()
 previous:=cell.snapshot.Load()
 next:=previous.distance
 at:=time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Microsecond)
 if next.UpdatedAt.Valid&&!at.After(next.UpdatedAt.Time){at=next.UpdatedAt.Time.Add(time.Microsecond)}
 if next.Latitude.Valid {next.TotalDistance+=calculateDistance(int(next.Latitude.Int64),int(next.Longitude.Int64),p.Latitude,p.Longitude)}
 next.Latitude=sql.NullInt64{Int64:int64(p.Latitude),Valid:true};next.Longitude=sql.NullInt64{Int64:int64(p.Longitude),Valid:true};next.UpdatedAt=sql.NullTime{Time:at,Valid:true}
 record:=coordinateRecord{Version:1,Generation:s.generation,ChairSequence:previous.sequence+1,Control:route.arrival(p),Location:ChairLocation{ID:ulid.Make().String(),ChairID:chairID,Latitude:p.Latitude,Longitude:p.Longitude,CreatedAt:at},Distance:next}
 if record.Control {record.Route=&route}
 var timing *coordinateTiming
 if s.coordinateCalls.Add(1)%coordinateSampleEvery==1 {timing=&coordinateTiming{started:time.Now(),locked:time.Now()}}
 stripe:=&s.coordinates.stripes[coordinateStripeIndex(chairID)]
 stripe.mu.Lock()
 record.Sequence=stripe.sequence+1
 err:=stripe.write(record,timing)
 if err==nil {stripe.sequence=record.Sequence;publishCoordinateSnapshot(cell,next,record.ChairSequence,timing)}else{s.failCoordinates(err)}
 if err==nil&&record.Control {cell.pending=append(cell.pending,record)}
 finishCoordinateHold(timing)
 stripe.metrics.finish(timing,err==nil,true,err==nil,previous.distance,next,route.Stage)
 stripe.mu.Unlock()
 if err!=nil{return time.Time{},err}
 s.owner.dirty.Store(chairID,cell.snapshot.Load())
 if err:=s.deliverArrivals(cell);err!=nil{return time.Time{},err}
 return at,nil
}
// Caller holds cell.mu. The arrival frame is the durable outbox entry; no
// successful coordinate response can precede the corresponding control commit.
func (s *rideState) deliverArrivals(cell *coordinateCell) error {
 for len(cell.pending)>0 {
  record:=cell.pending[0]
  var route coordinateRoute
  if err:=s.owner.call(context.Background(),http.MethodPost,"/arrival",ownerArrival{Generation:s.generation,Record:record},&route);err!=nil{return err}
  cell.pending=cell.pending[1:]
  cell.routeMu.Lock();if route.Sequence>=cell.route.Sequence {cell.route=route};cell.routeMu.Unlock()
 }
 return nil
}
func (s *rideState) applyProjection(batch ownerProjectionBatch) error {
 s.mu.Lock();defer s.mu.Unlock()
 if batch.Generation!=s.generation{return stateError(http.StatusConflict,"owner generation mismatch")}
 if !s.ready{return errors.New("control owner is unavailable")}
 positions:=make([]coordinateProjection,0,len(batch.Positions))
 for _,position:=range batch.Positions {
  cell:=s.coordinateCell(position.Distance.ChairID)
  if cell==nil{return errors.New("projection references missing chair")}
  old:=cell.snapshot.Load()
  if position.Sequence>old.sequence {positions=append(positions,position)}
 }
 if len(positions)==0{return nil}
 return s.commit(stateRecord{Projections:positions})
}
func (s *rideState) applyArrival(event ownerArrival) (coordinateRoute,error) {
 s.mu.Lock();defer s.mu.Unlock()
 record:=event.Record
 if event.Generation!=s.generation||record.Generation!=s.generation{return coordinateRoute{},stateError(http.StatusConflict,"owner generation mismatch")}
 ref:=coordinateReference{ChairID:record.Location.ChairID,ChairSequence:record.ChairSequence,StripeSequence:record.Sequence}
 cell:=s.coordinateCell(ref.ChairID)
 if cell==nil{return coordinateRoute{},errors.New("arrival references missing chair")}
 routeResult:=func() coordinateRoute {cell.routeMu.RLock();defer cell.routeMu.RUnlock();return cell.route}
 if s.arrivalRefs[ref] {return routeResult(),nil}
 if record.Route==nil||!record.Control||!record.Route.arrival(Coordinate{record.Location.Latitude,record.Location.Longitude}) {return coordinateRoute{},errors.New("invalid arrival event")}
 ride:=s.rides[record.Route.RideID]
 if ride==nil||ride.ChairID.String!=ref.ChairID||s.latest(ride)!=record.Route.Status {return coordinateRoute{},errors.New("arrival ride order mismatch")}
 if record.ChairSequence<=cell.snapshot.Load().sequence{return coordinateRoute{},errors.New("arrival was overtaken by projection")}
 status:="PICKUP";if record.Route.Status=="CARRYING"{status="ARRIVED"}
 if err:=s.commit(stateRecord{Coordinate:&ref,Projections:[]coordinateProjection{{Distance:record.Distance,Sequence:record.ChairSequence}},Statuses:[]RideStatus{s.newStatus(ride,status,record.Location.CreatedAt)}});err!=nil{return coordinateRoute{},err}
 notifyRide(&ride.Ride)
 return routeResult(),nil
}
func (s *rideState) ownerHandler() http.Handler {
 mux:=http.NewServeMux()
 if s.owner.role=="control" {
  mux.HandleFunc("GET /seed",func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){stateLifecycle.RLock();defer stateLifecycle.RUnlock();if err:=s.checkReady();err!=nil{writeStateError(w,err);return};writeJSON(w,200,s.ownerSeed())})
  mux.HandleFunc("POST /projection",func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){stateLifecycle.RLock();defer stateLifecycle.RUnlock();var batch ownerProjectionBatch;if err:=bindJSON(r,&batch);err!=nil{writeError(w,400,err);return};if err:=s.applyProjection(batch);err!=nil{writeStateError(w,err);return};writeJSON(w,200,struct{}{})})
  mux.HandleFunc("POST /arrival",func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){stateLifecycle.RLock();defer stateLifecycle.RUnlock();var event ownerArrival;if err:=bindJSON(r,&event);err!=nil{writeError(w,400,err);return};route,err:=s.applyArrival(event);if err!=nil{writeStateError(w,err);return};writeJSON(w,200,route)})
  mux.HandleFunc("POST /status-commit",func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){stateLifecycle.RLock();defer stateLifecycle.RUnlock();var command ownerStatusCommand;if err:=bindJSON(r,&command);err!=nil{writeError(w,400,err);return}
   s.mu.RLock();generation:=s.generation;ride:=s.rides[command.RideID];already:=false
   if ride!=nil&&ride.ChairID.String==command.ChairID {for _,id:=range ride.Statuses{if s.statuses[id].Status==command.Status{already=true;break}}};s.mu.RUnlock()
   if command.Generation!=generation {writeError(w,409,errors.New("owner generation mismatch"));return}
   if !already {if err:=s.chairStatusLocal(command.ChairID,command.RideID,command.Status);err!=nil{writeStateError(w,err);return}}
   s.mu.RLock();row:=s.ownerChair(command.ChairID);s.mu.RUnlock();writeJSON(w,200,row.Route)
  })
 } else {
  mux.HandleFunc("POST /pause",func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){s.owner.life.Lock();defer s.owner.life.Unlock();s.coordinateReady.Store(false);writeJSON(w,200,struct{}{})})
  mux.HandleFunc("POST /reset",func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){s.owner.life.Lock();defer s.owner.life.Unlock();var seed ownerSeed;if err:=bindJSON(r,&seed);err!=nil{writeError(w,400,err);return};if err:=s.resetCoordinateOwner(seed);err!=nil{writeStateError(w,err);return};writeJSON(w,200,struct{}{})})
  mux.HandleFunc("POST /install",func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){s.owner.life.RLock();defer s.owner.life.RUnlock();var install ownerInstall;if err:=bindJSON(r,&install);err!=nil{writeError(w,400,err);return};if install.Generation!=s.generation{writeError(w,409,errors.New("owner generation mismatch"));return}
   for _,row:=range install.Chairs {
    s.publishCoordinateChair(row.Chair);cell:=s.coordinateCell(row.Chair.ID);cell.mu.Lock()
    err:=s.deliverArrivals(cell)
    if err==nil {cell.routeMu.Lock();if row.Route.Sequence>=cell.route.Sequence{cell.route=row.Route};cell.routeMu.Unlock()}
    cell.mu.Unlock();if err!=nil{writeStateError(w,err);return}
   };writeJSON(w,200,struct{}{})
  })
  mux.HandleFunc("POST /status",func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){s.owner.life.RLock();defer s.owner.life.RUnlock();var command ownerStatusCommand;if err:=bindJSON(r,&command);err!=nil{writeError(w,400,err);return};if command.Generation!=s.generation{writeError(w,409,errors.New("owner generation mismatch"));return}
   cell:=s.coordinateCell(command.ChairID);if cell==nil{writeError(w,404,sql.ErrNoRows);return};cell.mu.Lock();defer cell.mu.Unlock()
   if err:=s.deliverArrivals(cell);err!=nil{writeStateError(w,err);return}
   // Publish all positions preceding this status boundary before committing
   // the command. The coordinate gate stays held through route installation.
   snapshot:=cell.snapshot.Load()
   if err:=s.owner.call(r.Context(),http.MethodPost,"/projection",ownerProjectionBatch{Generation:s.generation,Positions:[]coordinateProjection{{Distance:snapshot.distance,Sequence:snapshot.sequence}}},nil);err!=nil{writeStateError(w,err);return}
   var route coordinateRoute
   if err:=s.owner.call(r.Context(),http.MethodPost,"/status-commit",command,&route);err!=nil{writeStateError(w,err);return}
   cell.routeMu.Lock();cell.route=route;cell.routeMu.Unlock();writeJSON(w,200,struct{}{})
  })
 }
 return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){ip,_,err:=net.SplitHostPort(r.RemoteAddr);if err!=nil|| (ip!=s.owner.peerIP&&ip!="127.0.0.1"&&ip!="::1"){w.WriteHeader(http.StatusForbidden);return};r.Body=http.MaxBytesReader(w,r.Body,4<<20);mux.ServeHTTP(w,r)})
}
func (s *rideState) startProjection() {
 o:=s.owner;o.stop,o.done=make(chan struct{}),make(chan struct{})
 go func(){defer close(o.done);ticker:=time.NewTicker(100*time.Millisecond);defer ticker.Stop();checks:=0
  for {select{case <-o.stop:return;case <-ticker.C:}
   // Check generation even while paused after an interrupted initialize.
   checks++;if checks%10==0 {var seed ownerSeed;if err:=o.call(context.Background(),http.MethodGet,"/seed",nil,&seed);err==nil{o.life.Lock();if seed.Generation!=s.generation{_ = s.resetCoordinateOwner(seed)};o.life.Unlock()}}
   o.life.RLock()
   if s.coordinateAvailable()!=nil{o.life.RUnlock();continue}
   batch:=ownerProjectionBatch{Generation:s.generation}
   snapshots:=map[string]*coordinateSnapshot{}
   o.dirty.Range(func(key,value any) bool {
    id:=key.(string);cell:=s.coordinateCell(id);if cell==nil{return true}
    if !cell.mu.TryLock(){return true}
    err:=s.deliverArrivals(cell)
    if err==nil {snapshot:=cell.snapshot.Load();snapshots[id]=snapshot;batch.Positions=append(batch.Positions,coordinateProjection{Distance:snapshot.distance,Sequence:snapshot.sequence})}
    cell.mu.Unlock();return len(batch.Positions)<256
   })
   if len(batch.Positions)>0 {if err:=o.call(context.Background(),http.MethodPost,"/projection",batch,nil);err==nil{for id,snapshot:=range snapshots{o.dirty.CompareAndDelete(id,snapshot)}}}
   if err:=s.coordinates.sync();err!=nil{s.failCoordinates(err)}
   o.life.RUnlock()
  }
 }()
}
func (s *rideState) stopProjection() {
 if s.owner!=nil&&s.owner.stop!=nil {close(s.owner.stop);<-s.owner.done;s.owner.stop=nil}
}

func startOwnerListener(s *rideState) (*http.Server,error) {
 listener,err:=net.Listen("tcp",s.owner.listen);if err!=nil{return nil,err}
 server:=&http.Server{Handler:s.ownerHandler(),ReadHeaderTimeout:5*time.Second}
 go func(){if err:=server.Serve(listener);err!=nil&&!errors.Is(err,http.ErrServerClosed){panic(fmt.Sprintf("owner listener: %v",err))}}()
 return server,nil
}
EOF

// Drain the captured dirty set in bounded WAL frames. No fixed per-tick quota

func (s *rideState) projectDirty() {

package main

import (
 "context"
 "errors"
 "io"
 "net/http"
 "net/http/httptest"
 "os"
 "sync"
 "sync/atomic"
 "testing"
 "time"
)

func ownerTestTransport(role string) *ownerTransport {
 return &ownerTransport{role:role,epoch:secureRandomStr(8),peerIP:"127.0.0.1",client:&http.Client{Timeout:time.Second}}
}
func ownerTestControl(t *testing.T,directory,generation string) *rideState {
 t.Helper()
 wal,err:=openStateWAL(directory);if err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 acks,err:=openDeliveryState(directory);if err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 s:=&rideState{owner:ownerTestTransport("control"),wal:wal,acks:acks,coordinates:&coordinateState{},generation:generation,collationWeights:testAccountWeights,
  chairs:map[string]Chair{},chairTokens:map[string]string{},modelSpeeds:map[string]int{"fast":2},stats:map[string]chairStatsTotal{},rides:map[string]*memoryRide{},statuses:map[string]RideStatus{},userRides:map[string][]string{},chairRides:map[string][]string{},appEvents:map[string]*notificationQueue{},chairEvents:map[string]*notificationQueue{},initialDistances:map[string]distanceTotal{},arrivalRefs:map[coordinateReference]bool{}}
 acks.state=s;s.resetAccounts();s.coordinates.reset();s.resetCurrentIndexes()
 s.addChair(Chair{ID:"c",AccessToken:"chair-token",IsActive:true,Model:"fast"})
 seedMemoryRide(s,"r","c",time.Now().Add(-time.Hour).Truncate(time.Second))
 if err:=s.recoverWAL();err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 s.rebuildIndexes();if err:=acks.recover(generation);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)};s.refreshDeliveryIndexes()
 s.ready=true;s.coordinateReady.Store(true)
 s.metrics=newRideMetrics();s.resetDispatchMetrics()
 t.Cleanup(func(){s.close()})
 return s
}
func ownerTestCoordinate(t *testing.T,directory string,seed ownerSeed) *rideState {
 t.Helper()
 if err:=os.MkdirAll(directory,0700);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 coordinates,err:=openCoordinateState(directory);if err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 s:=&rideState{owner:ownerTestTransport("coordinate"),coordinates:coordinates,collationWeights:testAccountWeights}
 if err:=s.resetCoordinateOwner(seed);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 t.Cleanup(func(){s.stopProjection();s.close()})
 return s
}
type ownerFixture struct {
 control *rideState
 coordinate *rideState
 current atomic.Pointer[rideState]
 failArrival atomic.Bool
 dropStatus atomic.Int64
 dropArrival atomic.Int64
 controlServer,coordinateServer *httptest.Server
 controlDirectory,coordinateDirectory string
}
func newOwnerFixture(t *testing.T) *ownerFixture {
 t.Helper()
 f:=&ownerFixture{controlDirectory:t.TempDir(),coordinateDirectory:t.TempDir()}
 f.control=ownerTestControl(t,f.controlDirectory,"owner-test");f.current.Store(f.control)
 f.controlServer=httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){
  if r.URL.Path=="/arrival" && f.failArrival.Load(){w.WriteHeader(503);return}
  drop:=r.URL.Path=="/status-commit"&&f.dropStatus.Load()>0 || r.URL.Path=="/arrival"&&f.dropArrival.Load()>0
  if drop {
   recorder:=httptest.NewRecorder();f.current.Load().ownerHandler().ServeHTTP(recorder,r)
   if r.URL.Path=="/arrival"{f.dropArrival.Add(-1)}else{f.dropStatus.Add(-1)}
   connection,_,err:=w.(http.Hijacker).Hijack();if err==nil{connection.Close()};return
  }
  f.current.Load().ownerHandler().ServeHTTP(w,r)
 }))
 f.coordinate=ownerTestCoordinate(t,f.coordinateDirectory,f.control.ownerSeed())
 f.coordinate.owner.peerURL=f.controlServer.URL
 f.coordinateServer=httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter,r *http.Request){f.coordinate.ownerHandler().ServeHTTP(w,r)}))
 f.control.owner.peerURL=f.coordinateServer.URL
 t.Cleanup(func(){f.coordinateServer.Close();f.controlServer.Close()})
 return f
}
func (f *ownerFixture) restartControl(t *testing.T,generation string) {
 t.Helper()
 if err:=f.control.close();err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 f.control=ownerTestControl(t,f.controlDirectory,generation)
 f.control.owner.peerURL=f.coordinateServer.URL;f.current.Store(f.control)
}
func (f *ownerFixture) restartCoordinate(t *testing.T) {
 t.Helper()
 if err:=f.coordinate.close();err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 f.coordinate=ownerTestCoordinate(t,f.coordinateDirectory,f.control.ownerSeed())
 f.coordinate.owner.peerURL=f.controlServer.URL
}
func ownerHistory(t *testing.T,s *rideState) []coordinateRecord {
 t.Helper();var rows []coordinateRecord
 for i:=range s.coordinates.stripes {
  file,err:=os.Open(s.coordinates.stripes[i].wal.Name());if err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
  if _,err:=readCoordinateRecord(file);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
  for {record,err:=readCoordinateRecord(file);if errors.Is(err,io.EOF){break};if err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)};rows=append(rows,record)}
  file.Close()
 }
 return rows
}
func TestOwnerNormalCoordinatesDoNotWaitForControl(t *testing.T) {
 f:=newOwnerFixture(t)
 f.control.mu.Lock()
 done:=make(chan error,1)
 go func(){for _,p:=range []Coordinate{{0,0},{1,2},{1,2},{4,5}} {if _,err:=f.coordinate.coordinate("c",p);err!=nil{done<-err;return}};done<-nil}()
 select {case err:=<-done:if err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)};case <-time.After(time.Second):f.control.mu.Unlock();t.Fatal("ordinary coordinate waited for control")}
 if f.control.chairDistance("c").UpdatedAt.Valid {f.control.mu.Unlock();t.Fatal("ordinary coordinates were transferred synchronously")}
 f.control.mu.Unlock()
 if len(ownerHistory(t,f.coordinate))!=4 {t.Fatal("full position history was coalesced")}
 f.coordinate.projectDirty()
 if f.control.chairDistance("c").TotalDistance!=9{t.Fatal("projection omitted movement")}
 f.restartCoordinate(t);f.coordinate.projectDirty()
 if len(ownerHistory(t,f.coordinate))!=4||f.coordinate.chairDistance("c").TotalDistance!=9{t.Fatal("coordinate restart lost accepted positions")}
 previous:=runtimeState;runtimeState=f.coordinate;defer func(){runtimeState=previous}()
 snapshot:=rideMeasurementSnapshot().Metrics
 if snapshot["snapshot_status"]!=0||snapshot["ride_metrics_ready"]!=1 {t.Fatalf("coordinate owner measurements unavailable: %v",snapshot)}
 for i:=range f.control.coordinates.stripes{if f.control.coordinates.stripes[i].wal!=nil{t.Fatal("control owner opened coordinate history WAL")}}
}
func TestOwnerStatusArrivalCompletionAndACKGating(t *testing.T) {
 f:=newOwnerFixture(t)
 if err:=f.control.chairStatus("c","r","ENROUTE");err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if _,err:=f.coordinate.coordinate("c",Coordinate{10,20});err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if f.control.latest(f.control.rides["r"])!="PICKUP" || f.control.chairDistance("c").Latitude.Int64!=10 {t.Fatal("pickup and arrival position were not published together")}
 if err:=f.control.chairStatus("c","r","CARRYING");err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if _,err:=f.coordinate.coordinate("c",Coordinate{30,40});err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if _,err:=f.control.complete("r",5);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 ids:=f.control.rides["r"].Statuses
 if len(ids)!=6{t.Fatal("transition history omitted or duplicated events")}
 for _,id:=range ids[1:] {if err:=f.control.acks.acknowledge(context.Background(),"chair",id);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}}
 f.control.mu.Lock();f.control.refreshDeliveryIndexes();_,available:=f.control.matchingChairs["c"];f.control.mu.Unlock()
 if available{t.Fatal("completion ACK bypassed an older pending notification")}
 if err:=f.control.acks.acknowledge(context.Background(),"chair",ids[0]);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 f.control.mu.Lock();f.control.refreshDeliveryIndexes();_,available=f.control.matchingChairs["c"];f.control.mu.Unlock()
 if !available{t.Fatal("durably delivered chair remained unavailable")}
 f.restartControl(t,"owner-test")
 if f.control.latest(f.control.rides["r"])!="COMPLETED"||f.control.chairDistance("c").TotalDistance!=40||f.control.chairEvents["c"].count.Load()!=0{t.Fatal("control or delivery restart lost accepted state")}
}
func TestOwnerArrivalOutboxAndDuplicateAfterRestart(t *testing.T) {
 for _,dropReply:=range []bool{false,true}{t.Run(map[bool]string{false:"coordinate_committed",true:"control_committed"}[dropReply],func(t *testing.T){
  f:=newOwnerFixture(t)
  if err:=f.control.chairStatus("c","r","ENROUTE");err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
  if dropReply{f.dropArrival.Store(2)}else{f.failArrival.Store(true)}
  if _,err:=f.coordinate.coordinate("c",Coordinate{10,20});err==nil{t.Fatal("missing control confirmation returned success")}
  if len(ownerHistory(t,f.coordinate))!=1{t.Fatal("prepared arrival missing from durable outbox")}
  f.failArrival.Store(false)
  if dropReply{f.restartControl(t,"owner-test")}else{f.restartCoordinate(t)}
  f.coordinate.projectDirty()
  if f.control.latest(f.control.rides["r"])!="PICKUP"||len(f.control.rides["r"].Statuses)!=3{t.Fatal("arrival resend lost or duplicated pickup")}
  f.restartCoordinate(t);f.coordinate.projectDirty()
  if len(f.control.rides["r"].Statuses)!=3||len(f.coordinate.coordinateCell("c").pending)!=0{t.Fatal("committed arrival replayed as a new event")}
 })}
}
func TestOwnerLostStatusReplyFencesNextCoordinate(t *testing.T) {
 f:=newOwnerFixture(t)
 // Both callback responses are lost after their control WAL commits. The
 // outer request retries too, exhausting four idempotent callback responses.
 f.dropStatus.Store(4)
 if err:=f.control.chairStatus("c","r","ENROUTE");err==nil{t.Fatal("lost status confirmation unexpectedly succeeded")}
 if f.control.latest(f.control.rides["r"])!="ENROUTE"{t.Fatal("test did not commit the one-sided status")}
 f.restartControl(t,"owner-test")
 if _,err:=f.coordinate.coordinate("c",Coordinate{10,20});err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if f.control.latest(f.control.rides["r"])!="PICKUP"||len(f.control.rides["r"].Statuses)!=3{t.Fatal("lost status route caused a missed or duplicate pickup")}
}
func TestOwnerGenerationResetAndPausedControlRestart(t *testing.T) {
 f:=newOwnerFixture(t)
 if _,err:=f.coordinate.coordinate("c",Coordinate{1,2});err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 old:=ownerArrival{Generation:"owner-test",Record:ownerHistory(t,f.coordinate)[0]}
 f.coordinate.coordinateReady.Store(false)
 f.restartControl(t,"owner-test")
 f.coordinate.startProjection()
 deadline:=time.Now().Add(3*time.Second)
 for !f.coordinate.coordinateReady.Load()&&time.Now().Before(deadline){time.Sleep(10*time.Millisecond)}
 if !f.coordinate.coordinateReady.Load(){t.Fatal("same-generation control restart did not release an interrupted init pause")}
 f.coordinate.stopProjection()
 f.restartControl(t,"new-generation")
 f.coordinate.owner.life.Lock();err:=f.coordinate.resetCoordinateOwner(f.control.ownerSeed());f.coordinate.owner.life.Unlock();if err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if _,err:=f.control.applyArrival(old);err==nil{t.Fatal("old-generation arrival accepted")}
 previous:=runtimeState;runtimeState=f.control;defer func(){runtimeState=previous}()
 if err:=acknowledgeNotificationGeneration(context.Background(),"chair","r-matching","owner-test");err==nil{t.Fatal("old-generation ACK accepted")}

 if err:=f.control.chairStatus("c","r","ENROUTE");err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}

 group.Wait()

 if err:=f.control.chairStatus("c","r","CARRYING");err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if _,err:=f.coordinate.coordinate("c",Coordinate{30,40});err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}

func TestDeliveryACKIndependentOfControlLock(t *testing.T) {
 s:=memoryTestState(t)
 s.addChair(Chair{ID:"c",IsActive:true});seedMemoryRide(s,"r","c",time.Now().Add(-time.Hour))
 previous:=runtimeState;runtimeState=s;defer func(){runtimeState=previous}()
 sequence:=s.sequence
 s.mu.Lock()
 done:=make(chan error,1)
 go func(){done<-acknowledgeNotification(context.Background(),"chair","r-matching")}()
 select{case err:=<-done:if err!=nil{s.mu.Unlock();t.Fatal(err)};case <-time.After(time.Second):s.mu.Unlock();t.Fatal("recipient ACK waited for control write lock")}
 s.mu.Unlock()
 if s.sequence!=sequence||s.chairEvents["c"].count.Load()!=0||s.statuses["r-matching"].ChairSentAt!=nil{t.Fatal("ACK rewrote shared control state or failed to publish delivery")}
}
func TestDeliveryBatchReplayAndFailedWAL(t *testing.T) {
 s:=memoryTestState(t)
 s.addChair(Chair{ID:"c",IsActive:true});seedMemoryRide(s,"r","c",time.Now().Add(-time.Hour))
 for i:=0;i<95;i++{s.addStatus(s.newStatus(s.rides["r"],"ENROUTE",time.Now()))}
 ids:=append([]string(nil),s.rides["r"].Statuses...)
 var group sync.WaitGroup
 begin:=make(chan struct{})
 for _,id:=range ids[1:] {group.Add(1);go func(id string){defer group.Done();<-begin;if err:=s.acks.acknowledge(context.Background(),"chair",id);err!=nil{t.Error(err)}}(id)}
 close(begin);group.Wait()
 if s.chairEvents["c"].count.Load()!=1{t.Fatal("out-of-order ACKs consumed the wrong pending events")}
 stripe:=&s.acks.stripes[coordinateStripeIndex(deliveryKey("chair","c"))]
 file,err:=os.Open(stripe.wal.Name());if err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)};defer file.Close()
 if _,err:=readWALRecord(file);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 frames,acks:=0,0
 for{record,err:=readWALRecord(file);if err==io.EOF{break};if err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)};frames++;acks+=len(record.Acks)}
 if frames>=95||acks!=95{t.Fatalf("ACKs were not batched: frames=%d acks=%d",frames,acks)}
 previous:=runtimeState;runtimeState=s;defer func(){runtimeState=previous}()
 events,err:=loadNotifications(context.Background(),"chair","c",ids[90])
 if err!=nil||len(events)!=6||events[0].eventID!=ids[0]||events[1].eventID!=ids[91]{t.Fatal("pending-prefix and delivered suffix replay order changed")}
 // Rebuild from immutable control status rows, then replay only recipient WAL.
 s.acks.reset();s.rebuildIndexes()
 if err:=s.acks.recover(s.generation);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if s.chairEvents["c"].count.Load()!=1||!s.notificationSent("chair",ids[95]){t.Fatal("recipient WAL recovery lost sparse delivery state")}
 if err:=stripe.wal.Close();err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if err:=s.acks.acknowledge(context.Background(),"chair",ids[0]);err==nil{t.Fatal("ACK succeeded after recipient WAL failure")}
 if s.chairEvents["c"].count.Load()!=1||s.notificationSent("chair",ids[0]){t.Fatal("failed ACK consumed an event")}
}

// Include immutable control event after-images in the same WAL append as the
// mutation. Position projections are coalesced; control events are not.
func (s *rideState) controlRoutes(record stateRecord) []ownerChair {
 ids:=map[string]struct{}{}
 if record.Chair!=nil{ids[record.Chair.ID]=struct{}{}}
 for _,row:=range record.Rides{if row.ChairID.Valid{ids[row.ChairID.String]=struct{}{}}}
 for _,status:=range record.Statuses{if ride:=s.rides[status.RideID];ride!=nil&&ride.ChairID.Valid{ids[ride.ChairID.String]=struct{}{}}}
 routes:=make([]ownerChair,0,len(ids))
 for id:=range ids {
  chair:=s.chairs[id];if record.Chair!=nil&&record.Chair.ID==id{chair=*record.Chair}
  var row Ride
  status:=""
  if history:=s.chairRides[id];len(history)>0{ride:=s.rides[history[len(history)-1]];row=ride.Ride;status=s.latest(ride)}
  for _,changed:=range record.Rides{if changed.ChairID.String==id{row=changed;if ride:=s.rides[row.ID];ride!=nil{status=s.latest(ride)}}}
  for _,changed:=range record.Statuses{if changed.RideID==row.ID{status=changed.Status}}
  route:=coordinateRoute{RideID:row.ID,Status:status,Sequence:record.Sequence}
  switch status {
  case "MATCHING":route.Stage=flowAssigned
  case "ENROUTE":route.Stage=flowEnroute;route.Latitude,route.Longitude=row.PickupLatitude,row.PickupLongitude
  case "PICKUP":route.Stage=flowPickup
  case "CARRYING":route.Stage=flowCarrying;route.Latitude,route.Longitude=row.DestinationLatitude,row.DestinationLongitude
  case "ARRIVED":route.Stage=flowArrived
  case "COMPLETED":route.Stage=flowReuse
  }
  routes=append(routes,ownerChair{Chair:chair,Route:route,Initial:s.initialDistances[id]})
 }
 return routes
}
EOF

func TestOwnerControlEventOutboxRecoveryAndSequence(t *testing.T) {
 f:=newOwnerFixture(t)
 if err:=f.control.chairStatus("c","r","ENROUTE");err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if _,err:=f.coordinate.coordinate("c",Coordinate{10,20});err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}

 if _,err:=f.coordinate.coordinate("c",Coordinate{30,40});err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 if _,err:=f.control.complete("r",5);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 f.restartControl(t,"owner-test")
 f.control.owner.eventsMu.Lock()
 events:=append([]ownerInstall(nil),f.control.owner.events...)
 f.control.owner.eventsMu.Unlock()
 var statuses []string
 last:=uint64(0)
 for _,event:=range events {for _,chair:=range event.Chairs{if chair.Route.Sequence<=last{t.Fatal("control event sequence regressed")};last=chair.Route.Sequence;statuses=append(statuses,chair.Route.Status)}}
 if len(statuses)!=5||statuses[0]!="ENROUTE"||statuses[4]!="COMPLETED"{t.Fatalf("durable control events omitted transitions: %v",statuses)}
 if err:=f.control.flushRoutes(context.Background());err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 cell:=f.coordinate.coordinateCell("c");cell.routeMu.RLock();route:=cell.route;cell.routeMu.RUnlock()
 if route.Status!="COMPLETED"||route.Sequence!=last{t.Fatal("control outbox replay failed to synchronize completion")}
 // An old assignment/status retry cannot roll back a newer installed route.
 if err:=f.control.owner.call(context.Background(),http.MethodPost,"/install",events[0],nil);err!=nil{t.Fatal(err)}
 cell.routeMu.RLock();status:=cell.route.Status;cell.routeMu.RUnlock()
 if status!="COMPLETED"{t.Fatal("late event replay regressed route")}
}

## ASSISTANT (Worked for 24s; 2026-10-11 11:41:32 +0900)

**この構成でスコアを上げる主案としては、私の見立てが外れました。** 座標と制御を分ける理由はありましたが、得点改善につながるという評価が甘かったです。

当初の根拠だった「Go CPUの33.6%を移せる」は、仕事の移動量です。削減量ではありません。共通ロックのACK待ち620本も瞬間の待機数で、それを解消したときの処理能力の増加までは示していませんでした。

分割すると、投影RPC、到着・statusの同期、両所有者の整合処理が増えます。移設先の3号機にはnginxもあり、そこで飽和しました。nginxを1号機へ移すとスコアは回復しましたが、今度は座標処理とnginxが1号機で競合しました。配置変更前の余裕も、処理量が増えれば減ります。この増加を十分に見込めていませんでした。

今回785,325点は元の784,884点とほぼ同じで、main相当の900,399点には届きません。**分割を維持する複雑さに見合う得点上の利点は、現時点ではありません。**

学ぶべき点は「分割が常に悪い」ではなく、**移せる仕事より、追加される仕事・同期待ち・移設先の容量を含めた差し引きを評価すべきだった**ということです。これ以上案1に固執せず、実績のあるmain構成を基準に進める判断が妥当です。
